*tattvānudhyāna-koṭir bhavatu bhavatu vā vaiṣṇavī bhakti-koṭiḥ*  
*koṭy-aṁśo ’py asya na syāt tad api guṇa-gaṇo yaḥ svataḥ-siddha āste*  
*śrīmāc-caitanyacandra-priya-caraṇa-nakha-jyotir-āmoda-bhājām*

**Let renunciation be multiplied millions of times! Let millions of virtues, beginning with peacefulness, sense control, tolerance and friendliness, be multiplied millions of times! Let there be millions of meditations on the words** ***tat tvam asi*****! Let there be devotion to Lord Viṣṇu multiplied millions of times! All these virtues taken together do not equal even a millionth part of the multitude of perfect transcendental qualities possessed by the great souls who find transcendental bliss in the splendor of the toenails of the dear devotees of Śrī Caitanyacandra.**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, if we had not met you, this wonderful description by Śrīla Prabodhānanda Sarasvatī would have sounded like exaggeration. But this description fits you so perfectly that hearing this poetry gives us bliss.

On top of all these wonderful spiritual qualities, you have unbounded compassion for the fallen souls of this world. You declared your mission in the same *Vaiśiṣṭyāṣṭakam* prayers:

61\. The Vaiṣṇavas are famous for being sympathetic towards others’ suffering,

And that fame will be increased by mature preaching.

62\. If eternally pure devotional service to Kṛṣṇa is awakened in everyone,

Kali will flee of his own accord, crying “Ha! Ha!”

63\. Mahāprabhu’s message is *prāṇinām upakārāya:* Act for the welfare of others. This is the storehouse of happiness both now and after death.

64\. So much work has fallen into your hands:

Everyone should join together and do something about it.

65\. *Vāsudeva-vipra* paid his obeisances to the Lord and said,

“Please deliver all the living beings.

66\. “Give all their sins to me, who am most fallen,

And relinquish all the suffering of the unhappy souls.”

67\. He was the best Vaiṣṇava, para*-duḥkha-duḥkhī.*

He found no happiness in gratifying his senses.

68\. What kind of compassion can the nondevotee show?

He simply commits offences with his *daridra-nārāyaṇa* philosophy.

69\. The Vaiṣṇava’s mercy is scientific. Without the mercy of the Vaiṣṇava,

The world is simply insurmountable Māyā.

70\. If there were a kingdom of Viṣṇu and Vaiṣṇavas on earth,

Then everyone would be happy, say the saintly persons and sages.

What you preached, you practiced. Based on these ideas of yours, you established ISKCON. You did it. You didn’t just speak empty words, but you backed them up by your actions. Sanātana Gosvāmī told Haridāsa Ṭhākura (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta Antya* 4.101–02):

*pratyaha kara tina-lakṣa nāma-saṅkīrtana*  
*sabāra āge kara nāmera mahimā kathana*

**“My dear sir, you are chanting the holy name 300,000 times daily and informing everyone of the importance of such chanting.**

*āpane ācare keha, nā kare pracāra*  
*pracāra karena keha, nā karena ācāra*

**“Some behave very well but do not preach the cult of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, whereas others preach but do not behave properly.**

*‘ācāra’, ‘pracāra’,—nāmera karaha ‘dui’ kārya*  
*tumi—sarva-guru, tumi jagatera ārya*

**“You simultaneously perform both duties in relation to the holy name by your personal behavior and by your preaching. Therefore you are the spiritual master of the entire world, for you are the most advanced devotee in the world.”**

Sanātana Gosvāmī clearly defines herein the bona fide spiritual master of the world. The qualifications expressed in this connection are that one must act according to the scriptural injunctions and at the same time preach. One who does so is a bona fide spiritual master. Haridāsa Ṭhākura was the ideal spiritual master because he regularly chanted on his beads a prescribed number of times. Indeed, he was chanting the holy name of the Lord 300,000 times a day. Similarly, the members of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement chant a minimum of sixteen rounds a day, which can be done without difficulty, and at the same time they must preach the cult of Caitanya Mahāprabhu according to the gospel of *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is*. One who does so is quite fit to become a spiritual master for the entire world.

We are indeed the most fortunate persons to have such a perfect spiritual master, as described in this purport. Now our only prayers to you are those you wrote in the last two statements of your *Vaiśiṣṭyāṣṭakam:*

79\. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please be merciful today. Have compassion. You are not a conditioned soul of this material world.

80\. Whatever independence we have, let it be cast to the waters. The lowly Abhay offers this presentation today.

We walked with you and talked with you as if we were your associates, but actually you were a Vaikuṇṭha man, a Goloka man, and we are lost souls in the material world. Normally we would have no qualifications to be around you, but since you are *para-duḥkha-duḥkhī,* we beg you to keep us under the cool shade of your lotus feet and allow us to appear again and again wherever you are.

Begging to remain your eternal servants life after life,  
The devotees in ISKCON Ahmedabad, India.  
(written by Yaśomatīnandana Dāsa)

## Aravade

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet!

This year, on December 9th, we plan to celebrate the grand opening of the new temple project of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopāl and Kṛṣṇa-tattva Darshan, an educational and spiritual diorama exhibition.

As we mentioned in last year’s Vyāsa-pūjā offering, ISKCON Aravade is a center situated in a very small village of five thousand people. Still, we are building a medium-size temple, with a budget of Rs.20 million. This beautiful temple will have Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopāl Deities on the main altar and an *utsava-mūrti* of Gaura-Nitāi. There will also be many *mūrtis* and full-figure sculptures and bas-relief of Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes on the outside wall of the temple, on the *parikramā* path. In the front of the temple will be a Kāliya-Kṛṣṇa fountain within a big Vṛndāvana garden.

Every year more than 20,000 people attend the Hare Krishna festival here. But for the grand opening we expect more than 50,000, including ISKCON devotees from around the world.

Devotees in our center are on a marathon to preach and collect the funds for completing the temple project. Certainly, only with the boost of your mercy we will succeed. In this effort we are preaching to the leaders of the society, such as ministers of this state. In this we are following on your footsteps.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we know that you will be here in the temple, but we want you to stay here very happily. Only by your presence will we able to stay together in ISKCON. This is possible only when we co-operate and follow all the rules and regulation given by you. We pray from our hearts for your kind mercy, so that on this very auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā we may again remind ourselves of your instructions and vow to follow them with our life and soul.

Thanking you,  
Your servants at ISKCON Aravade, India.

## Bahia

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humblest and most respectful obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet!

On this day we gather together to try to express the feelings of gratitude we should have toward you. I say “we should have” because we cannot truly understand the gigantic blessings you have given us through your herculaean preaching efforts, your patience, your dedication, and, most of all, your compassion. There is no one in the three worlds who can give to somebody something more valuable than what you gave to us.

In the present degraded age, when we are threatened from every side, when even the concept of sprituality is most often corrupted, what would have been our fate without the blessings you gave us? If we could truly understand the magnitude of your blessings, we would know that there is nothing else for us to do but distribute your books or in some way help your book distribution.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, forgive us for not yet having inundated the world with your books. Because this is our obligation, forgive us, for we still haven’t absorbed ourselves in the service of your lotus feet completely. So please give us your blessings so we can do it and thus fully express our gratitude to you for your mercy upon us.

We ask your blessings because we are not able to do it on our own. Only through your mercy can this and much more be done. Thank you, thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Wishing to achieve your blessings to became expert at serving the lotus feet of your servants,  
Your faithful followers and great admirers at ISKCON Bahia, Brazil.

## Bali

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our most respectful obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the savior of the fallen souls in this material world. Without your kindness, we would never have known our real goal of life as human beings, nor would we ever have known that the Supreme Personality of Godhead is Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your kindness, many of your disciples have come to Indonesia and Bali to give their holy association and spread the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. By the effort of your disciples to guide us, we can do devotional service to Śrī Kṛṣṇa, although in 1984 our movement was banned in Indonesia. But because we have faith that everything is controlled by the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and that what we are learning in Kṛṣṇa consciousness is the supreme truth, we have continued to do devotional service while remaining very careful. And the fact is that now more and more people are joining this *saṅkīrtana* movement. In fact, by the mercy of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, in Bali we have some temples because the new devotees keep increasing.

On this most auspicious day we would like to report that at present we are building our Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurāṅga Temple; it is just about 70% finished. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are praying that we can complete this project.

The *brahmacārīs* perform the daily activities of the temple *sādhana:* regular *kīrtana* in the morning and evening. We hold the weekly feast and on Wednesday, when we have a cultural presentation. We also hold a *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class for the congregational devotees every Thursday. Also, we have some cows, although they don’t give milk, as is typical for Balinese cows.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, that’s all we can present to you as our humble offering. We pray for your mercy and the mercy of your disciples whom you have kindly sent here, so that you and they may continue to guide us in our attempt to progress in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda. May your glories be proclaimed throughout the three worlds.

Your grandchildren at Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurāṅga Temple, Badung, Bali, Indonesia.  
(written by Sureśa Dāsa)

## Baroda

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you!

We are seeing a revolution in India, with many people becoming devotees.

Even in the USA one meets many an Indian who has some cousin, friend, or family member connected to this movement by You Divine Grace’s mercy. Once my devotee friend in Orlando, Florida, called his bank to open an account. The person who answered heard *kīrtana* in the background and said, “Hare Kṛṣṇa, Prabhu,” and told him that he was also a devotee in your movement and that he was working in an Indian call center.

One day judges will wear *tilaka,* and that day is coming soon. It is you who started this movement and you set in stone the very strong foundation upon which your disciples stand while carrying on this movement. The surge of this movement is certainly a testimony to the efforts of those who follow your instructions. The devotees in your ISKCON are sincere. Indeed, their sincerity is the heart of ISKCON, and with that sincerity comes enthusiasm to give others what they have lost.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the foundation you have given us is like the Himalayas.

Your servants at ISKCON Baroda, India.  
(written by Bhakta Piyush Mehta)

## Beed

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you!

Today, on your Vyāsa-pūjā day, all we can offer you is our gratitude for going all over the world to bring everyone this wonderful process of *bhakti-yoga,* centered on the chanting of the holy names of the Lord. Without your mercy and compassion, we wouldn’t even know who Kṛṣṇa is and how to go to Him.

Śrīla Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!!

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we here at ISKCON Beed are daily striving to practice the science you gave us. We are becoming purified, and we pray that you will keep us under you wing forever so that we will always be engaged in trying to please Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindajī.

O most merciful Vaiṣṇava! Where would we be without you? If not for you, how could it ever have been possible for us to become free from the hellish life of this material world? Please be merciful to us and give us the shelter of your lotus feet. Without your mercy we are lost and completely unable to obtain the mercy of Kṛṣṇa.

You once said that it is impossible for a disciple to repay the debt to his spiritual master. We therefore beg at your lotus feet that you will demand from us that we eternally make payments on this unpayable debt, just as Ananta Śeṣa is eternally speaking the glories of Śrī Kṛṣṇa without ever being able to finish.

We know that you have given us the best, and that the only flaw is our weakness of heart, so please, beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, keep us with you always.

We are striving to extend you mercy to our brothers and sisters in Beed, and we will continue to preach this wonderful message of eternal life, knowledge, and bliss as long as we live. May you be strongly present in our hearts and minds so we can penetrate the ignorance of our own hearts and the hearts of others.

Thank you so much, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and the most happy Vyāsa-pūjā day to you.

Your sons and daughters at ISKCON Beed, Maharashtra, India.

## Bhaktivedanta Manor

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

A few months ago your servants here at Bhaktivedanta Manor received a visit from the servants of the Queen. Known as the Masters of the Royal Household, their daily services, whether at Buckingham Palace or Windsor Castle, involve seeing to all the personal and domestic needs of the Queen and her husband, Prince Philip. They organize all the cleaning of hundreds of rooms, the catering of daily meals and state banquets, the royal wardrobe, the decoration of the palace, and the gardening, security and travel.

After giving them a tour, during which they commented very favorably on the temple room, your personal quarters, the gardens and the cows, we were comparing notes on what it means to be a servant. They agreed that it means to have no independent desires, other than the service of the master. That a true servant always thinks of those he serves, and what they need—and that true service is all about getting the details right.

They were quite surprised that people outside the royal household even talk in these terms, since the word “servant” has now become an antiquated term, not generally used. We talked some more and then served them delicious *mahā-prasādam*. Although it wasn’t served on the finest crockery, with knives and forks made of pure silver placed in just the right order, they enjoyed it very much, asking for second helpings. They were touched by the fact that someone understood them and their life of service, and that now they themselves were being served by others—offered delicious food that was, somehow, different from anything they’d tasted before.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have taken us members of the British Empire and introduced us to the happiness and fulfillment of service. Not just any service, however, but divine service that is rewarded not only here in this life but for eternity. It is a paradoxical yet verifiable truth that in a world where everyone wants the sweet taste of being a master of others, the real delight is in service to God and His pure devotee.

Bhaktivedanta Manor is your house, Śrīla Prabhupāda, where you eternally serve Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda, and we are your faithful servants. You are everywhere here at the Manor. In every room we serve you and try to be always conscious of everything you wanted this temple, its grounds, and its ever-growing community of devotees to be.

Our devotee community has grown so much in size and diversity simply because we have offered to others what you originally gave us. Thousands now accept you as their *ācārya* and follow your instructions in their daily life.

This year, to serve them all better, to help those who do not have the advantage of living inside a temple—now by far the majority—we have formed eight teams of devotees to help everyone in the community move forward in their spiritual life. We have styled it The Year of the Congregation, and in our service to you we are trying to get the details right. You commented once that you didn’t want us to make you into another Alexander the Great, who only imagined that he’d conquered so much territory but factually left so many hearts and lives unchanged. So we are trying to ensure that everyone who becomes a devotee actually remains a devotee, despite all the trying circumstances of life.

First, we want to make sure that everyone is reading and understanding your teachings by becoming students of your books. We’re attempting to create a graduated system of accredited study of your main books. And we’re arranging that more and more classes are held where your books are thoroughly discussed.

Equally important, we felt, was to ensure that all our community members had access to a caring and affectionate senior Vaiṣṇava with whom they could discuss their challenges. Such a spiritual guide should try to spend time with the devotees just as you did, offering time for private conversation and *iṣṭagoṣṭhī*.

In 1973 you wrote to us here in England that we should do six things to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the country: (1) perform *harināma-saṅkīrtana,* (2) give some free information, (3) distribute your books, (4) give some *prasādam*, at least “some small thing”, (5) answer the public’s questions, and (6) arrange programs in a home, school, or hall. These simple activities started the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement in this country, and its continued growth is dependent on just the same six items.

So this year we are determined that not only our temple devotees but every community member has an opportunity to experience the thrill of being a preacher—to go out on *saṅkīrtana*, hold programs in homes and halls, and feel the personal responsibility for expanding your mission.

Our community has grown so big that sometimes the members of constituent groups don’t get to talk with one another. This has sometimes led to misunderstandings. Because you wanted ISKCON to be one family—yet to have all the constituent groups function as autonomous units—we have taken steps to improve communications and cooperation between ourselves.

You stated that Kṛṣṇa consciousness is not a matter of imaginative ecstasy or armchair speculation but is based on practical acts of devotion. More than ever this year, we are making such practical devotional service available for teams of volunteers. Hundreds are now coming forward to help with planting crops, cow care, cooking, hosting of guests, educating, preaching, and book distribution.

You taught that our members should come to the temple to learn what to do at home. That by sitting together with friends and relatives, all members can increase their devotion through singing together, discussing the scriptures, offering *ārati* to the household altar, and taking *prasādam*. Although there are already many such regular gatherings where these basic activities of devotion take place, we are trying very hard this year to expand the number. When these gatherings actually take place in all the towns and villages that make up the huge London conurbation, we know that we will have begun to fulfill your order.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have a team now that designs and publishes Kṛṣṇa conscious literature using contemporary artwork. The aim is to use the best of technology to not only reach out to new audiences, of all different ages, but to produce resources to inform and help them along the way once they’ve become interested.

By your repeated insistence, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are counting the books that are distributed, but also the members and new devotees made, the programs held, and the number of homes where *kīrtana* and class, *ārati* and *prasādam,* are shared with gatherings of devotees on a regular basis.

We are committed to assisting you with your mission to bring Lord Kṛṣṇa into the lives of everyone, to introduce the Vedic literature as the medicine for the ills of the age, and to spread the saving grace of the *mahā-mantra* far and wide. And in order to do that we want to make your movement not only well organized, but guided with intelligence.

We hope that these details will please Your Divine Grace, and that they are at least the beginning of a way to show you our gratitude for the inconceivable compassion you have shown us.

The devotees of the Bhaktivedanta Manor Community, UK.  
(written by Kṛpāmaya Dāsa)

## Brahmapur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Respected Śrīla Prabhupāda,

By your abundant mercy, our center is progressing well. It was by your direction that we purchased this land for spreading Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s message. We have already built a kitchen, as well as three rooms and to accommodate the *brahmacārīs*. We are holding *saṅkīrtana* programs every Sunday, and also once a month on the Saṅkrānti day. These programs are getting a good response from the general public. Two new students have joined to do full-time devotional service at your lotus feet. Slowly we are all trying our best to fulfill your desire to distribute your books.

The devotees at ISKCON Brahmapur, India.

## Brampton

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept millions of our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Dāmodara!

We were wondering how, of all the most fallen people, we ended up in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and then this verse came to mind from the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Madhya* 19.151):

*brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva*  
*guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja*

**“According to their karma, all living entities are wandering throughout the entire universe. Some of them are being elevated to the upper planetary systems, and some are going down into the lower planetary systems. Out of many millions of wandering living entities, one who is very fortunate gets an opportunity to associate with a bona fide spiritual master by the grace of Kṛṣṇa. By the mercy of both Kṛṣṇa and the spiritual master, such a person receives the seed of the creeper of devotional service.”**

We become devotees of Kṛṣṇa not necessarily by our own endeavor but through the unconditional love and mercy that our spiritual master bestows upon us, pulling us out of the clutches of *māyā*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have freed us from captivity and bondage and given a true purpose to our lives: to further the message of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa through the *guru-paramparā*system. Śrīla Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja Gosvāmī very clearly states that the instructing spiritual master is a bona fide representative of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

*śikṣā-guruke ta’ jāni kṛṣṇera svarūpa*  
*antaryāmī, bhakta-śreṣṭha,—ei dui rūpa*

**“One should know the instructing spiritual master to be the Personality of Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa manifests Himself as the Supersoul and as the greatest devotee of the Lord.” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi*** **1.47)**

Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, the most merciful incarnation of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, transmitted the essence of Vedic knowledge by means of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra,* and as Lord Caitanya Himself explained to Prakāśānanda Sarasvatī:

My spiritual master considered Me a fool, and therefore he chastised Me. “You are a fool,” he said. “You are not qualified to study Vedānta philosophy, and therefore You must always chant the holy name of Kṛṣṇa. This is the essence of all mantras, or Vedic hymns.” (*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi* 7.71–72)

You also gave us the prime benediction, the chanting of the holy name. So what qualification do we have to glorify you, O Prabhupāda, the most benevolent and worthy transmitter of transcendental knowledge of Śrī Kṛṣṇa?

We offer our deepest gratitude to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. With our meager efforts we hope to help show the world the path back to Godhead. We have had our share of struggles over the last two years since the inception of this center; however, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you continue to shower your unconditional mercy upon us.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please forgive us for any offenses we committed during this struggle. We beg at your lotus feet, with straws between our teeth, to give us the spiritual wisdom to become more humble, austere, truthful, tolerant, and compassionate toward all living entities as we progress in the service of fulfilling your blessed mission.

All glories to all the Vaiṣṇavas! Hare Kṛṣṇa! *Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

The eternal servants of your servants at ISKCON Brampton, Canada.

## Brisbane

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

We, your servants at Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi Mandira, express to you our deepest gratitude on this most auspicious day of your appearance and pray for your continued mercy in our lives, for without it how can we survive in this darkest of ages?

Like the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, you are a sun who has arisen to disperse the dense darkness of Kali-yuga. You have arisen in the East and have brought to us in the West the life-giving light of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Making us into *sūrya-kānta* gems, which are invested with a portion of the sun’s light, you have empowered us and used us in distributing this light of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to others. Just as the sun purifies even the filthiest of places with its powerful rays, you purify and uplift even the lowest of men with your mercy. And just like the sun, you continually inspire our intelligence (*dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayāt*), thus giving us the way to implement your instructions. Please always keep shining on us and illuminate for us and the whole word the path to perfection.

Your servants at ISKCON Brisbane, Australia.

## Budapest

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to you!

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

What does the world know about such a small country as Hungary? It is a tiny spot on the map, and although the earth is only a small planet in this universe, this country is just a little hidden part of it. And anyway, why is it important to know anything about this country? Especially its capital, Budapest? Small country, with an even smaller capital . . .

However, an insignificant place can become significant by the will of a great personality. Without you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, this place would have remained forever the capital of a meaningless country merged in the sinful activities of Kali-yuga. But as a sunbeam may break through a small hole in a thick wall, your holy will is shining over this place. By your mercy, wonderful things are manifesting that surpass the thoughts of our mundane mind. As we know, anything is possible in the spiritual world. Therefore, if the desire of a sincere and pure devotee is manifested, it cannot be limited by mundane ideas. By one’s following and accepting such a personality, fame will come. You gave us the opportunity, and we are trying to take it. As you wrote in your purport to *Bhagavad-gītā* 10.4–5:

. . . Lord Caitanya \[said\] that a man is famous when he is known as a great devotee. That is real fame. If one has become a great man in Kṛṣṇa consciousness and it is known, then he is truly famous.

We are trying to follow your footsteps. A beautiful temple has been built here in Budapest, many, many devotees go out every day to give your books to the people, preaching and education are improving, and several hundred portions of *prasādam* are distributed daily. People know Kṛṣṇa consciousness here, and they like the devotees. But this is all your merit, for we cannot give others anything else but what we have received from you. By all our efforts you are glorified and your fame is recognized. And every day is another special chance for us to be with you. The love and appreciation we receive from the people always remind us of you, our great *ācārya*. We are famous only due to you. We are trying to become devotees and be worthy of this glory.

Actually, this country and this city are nothing without you. Without you they would not be worth knowing at all. Budapest is only the small capital of a small country . . .

Please bless us on this holy day so that we can make the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement famous, even in such an insignificant place.

Your servants,  
The devotees of the Hare Kṛṣṇa temple in Budapest, Hungary.

## Chamorshi

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

My dear Guru Mahārāja,

Please accept my most humble obeisances at the dust of your divine lotus feet. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Today is the most auspicious day of your appearance, and for me it is a very holy and meaningful day. But for me to offer you something worthy is not possible, since I am the most poor and fallen of all your disciples.

You are the representative of Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Your appearance is a perfect example of Lord Caitanya’s kindness and mercy on the fallen living entities. Lord Caitanya said,

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*  
*\[CB Antya-khaṇḍa 4.126\]*

**“In every town and village on this earth the glories of My name will be chanted.”**

His divine prophecy was fulfilled by you. You’re a Vaiṣṇava *cintāmaṇi* gem. You have not infused countless towns and villages with *kṛṣṇa-bhakti* by your transcendental touch, but also the entire Western world today reverberates with the Hare Kṛṣṇa chant because of you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I am your insignificant spiritual son. Please bestow your blessings on me for my future spiritual life. I am carrying on with my religious life only by your saving grace. When at times I reflect on how the ISKCON’s Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāmasundara temple at Kṛṣṇa Nagar, Chamorshi, has come along over the years, I see it only as due to your support and kindness.

This was a forest, with only secluded tribes living around the place. After the Hare Kṛṣṇa community came here, the religious, social, and economic conditions have improved. The people here may be very poor, but they are very deeply devoted Vaiṣṇavas at the same time.

Here is a summary of the spiritual programs conducted at Chamorshi: (1) the Gopī- sabhā for women, 150–200 of whom attend every Friday; (2) the Bhāgavata-sabhā, which 300–400 devotees attend every Sunday; (3) regular home programs; (4) the annual Nityānanda-trayodaśī festival, which includes (a) *akhaṇḍa-nāma-saṅkīrtana* for three days, (b) a *bhāgavata-saptāha*, (c) and an *anna-satra*, a massive feast for one and all; (5) tourism; (6) village programs; (7) development of the old temple; (8) organic farming; (9) cow protection; (10) Janmāṣṭamī, Jhulana-yātrā, and Govardhana Pūjā, all celebrated with great fervor.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless me and the devotees in Chamorshi so we can make steady progress in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Your servants at ISKCON Chamorshi, India.  
(written by Parameśvara Dāsa)

## Chandigarh

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. The more we meditate on the famous prediction of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu—*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma/ sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*—the more we become aware that you are the personality who was specially sent by Him to fulfill that prediction. \[*CB Antya-khaṇḍa* 4.126\]

Due to Kṛṣṇa’s personal request, you came to rescue the fallen souls from this material world and take them back home, back to Godhead. You accomplished that mission perfectly, and before leaving this planet you made a perfect arrangement through your ISKCON to continue that mission.

You are a perfect saint, Śrīla Prabhupāda. A saint is one who can change the nature (*svabhāva*) of a person. That person is not a saint who can produce ashes in the air or change a banana into an orange or an apple. What use is that? If one can change a sinner into a devotee, that is real benefit. You did exactly that. And not just one or two sinners. Your Divine Grace converted thousands of meat-eaters into Vaiṣṇavas. It was not easy. It was a miracle. Sārvabhauma Bhaṭṭācārya, describing his own case, said about Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu:

*mahāprabhu vinā keha nāhi dayāmaya*  
*kākere garuḍa kare,—aiche kon haya*

**“Who can be more merciful than Caitanya Mahāprabhu? He has changed a crow into a Garuḍa.”**

As the perfect follower of Caitanya Mahāprabhu, He empowered you to convert thousands of crowlike persons into pure devotees of Kṛṣṇa like Garuḍa.

It is such a difficult task in this Age of Kali. This is the age of *adharma,* and Kali is the ruler of this age. With the help of Sin and his army, consisting of soldiers like Lust, Greed, and Anger, he has already conquered the entire world. Practically everyone has become his loyal subject. However, you came like a valiant knight and waged a war against him. Alone you stood against the entire world. In Dvāpara-yuga Kṛṣṇa did that when He stood up against Kaṁsa, but Balarāma was with Him. You, however, started your war alone, against the formidable army of Kali. You went right into the heart of the enemy camp and challenged your enemy to come out and fight. Right in front of Kali’s eyes you opened the door of his prison, and you not only rescued his prisoners but recruited them to form your army to fight against Kali.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your mercy we distributed more than 21,000 copies of *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* during the last December marathon (2007), which is a record for ISKCON Chandigarh. We were Number 1 in ISKCON for the book distribution marathon in our category (small temples). All devotees, whether from the temple, the congregation, or the ISKCON Youth Forum (I.Y.F.), were inspired by His Holiness Gopāl Krishna Goswami to put their hearts and consciousness into achieving this landmark. The devotees went to corporate offices and plants, colleges, marketplaces, bus stops, railway stations, exhibitions, and private homes to distribute your books in an effort to remove the darkness of this Age of Kali.

We feel very happy to report that four youths, including two engineers and one doctor, have joined ISKCON Chandigarh full-time after leaving their lucrative jobs. Our ISKCON Youth Forum is growing steadily, thus bringing more inquisitive young persons to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You always encouraged university and college preaching. We are regularly preaching in two leading engineering colleges in Punjab, and with your blessings we hope to inspire many educated youths to take up the cause of your mission wholeheartedly.

We also have an IYF for girls, Śrīla Prabhupāda. They hold their weekly seminars on spiritual topics and participate enthusiastically in various services, including preaching at festivals and distributing books during the book marathon.

You will be pleased to know, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that, inspired by the teachings of Prahlāda Mahārāja, on Nṛsiṁha-caturdaśī 2007 we started two Bhakta Prahlāda Schools—one in your temple in Chandigarh and one in a devotee’s house in the nearby town of Panchlula. Meeting weekly, the schools provide children aged six to thirteen with spiritual and moral education based on your instructions. The parents are overjoyed. About eighty children are benefiting from these transcendental programs.

On Janmāṣṭamī, Gaura Pūrṇimā, and other major holidays the young boys and girls of the IYF and the Bhakta Prahlāda Schools present very good ballets, dramas, and skits based on the scripture. These performances attract a lot of people to Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Our aim is to please you by strengthening the congregation and developing our preaching to youth and children more and more, which has a bright prospect in Chandigarh.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bestow upon us this one benediction: that we will have unflinching faith in your words and will engage in uninterrupted service to them. Without this, our love for you will merely be a sentimental, psychological phenomenon.

Aspiring to become your instruments,  
The devotees at ISKCON Chandigarh, India.

## Coventry

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**A Song for Prabhupāda**

Prabhupāda, we stand before you.  
You’ve touched all our hearts.  
A solitary soldier of Gaurāṅga,  
You really are the best.  
\[Chorus\] Like a star, you truly shine. Thank you for giving us ISKCON!  
We look at your life and what you’ve been through.  
Prabhupāda, we’re indebted to you.  
A pure soul, you’re one of a few.  
Prabhupāda, all glories to you!  
From country to country you spread Kṛṣṇa’s name.  
Kṛṣṇa’s name became a shelter for us all.  
No one can do what you have done.  
You have endless qualities.  
\[Chorus\] Like a star, you truly shine. Thank you for giving us ISKCON!  
We look at your life and what you’ve been through.  
Prabhupāda, we’re indebted to you.  
A pure soul, you’re one of a few.  
Prabhupāda, all glories to you!  
You were loved wherever you went,  
You still are.  
You’re irreplaceable, Prabhupāda.  
Our shining star!  
\[Chorus\] Like a star, you truly shine. Thank you for giving us ISKCON!  
We look at your life and what you’ve been through.  
Prabhupāda, we’re indebted to you.  
A pure soul, you’re one of a few.  
Prabhupāda, all glories to you!  
We really love you, Prabhupāda, we love you!

Your servants at ISKCON Coventry, UK.

## Dallas

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**“I was born in the darkest ignorance, and my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torch of knowledge. I offer my respectful obeisances unto him.”**

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

**“By the mercy of the guru, even a dumb man can become the greatest orator and even a lame man can cross mountains. I offer my obeisances to such a guru, the deliverer of the fallen.”**

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept the most prostrated *sarvāṅga-daṇḍavat praṇāmas* that our hearts can command at the dust of Your Divine Grace’s most magnanimous and divine lotus feet, on this the most auspicious and holy occasion of the 112th anniversary of your appearance.

All glories to Your Divine Grace, and all glories to your Hare Kṛṣṇa movement, ISKCON, which is full of variegatedness!

As our most loving father, grandfather. and great-grandfather, please accept the following simple offering as an expression of our heartfelt love and appreciation for you—an offering made possible by your divine grace and mercy.Śrīla Prabhupāda, you expressed caring concern about how the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement you so painstakingly and expertly set up would continue after the departure of your *vapu* from this planet. As one of the countless number of expressions of love you have inspired in your affectionate followers, please accept the profound and exhilarating appreciation and taste our youth here have for, amongst other services, engaging in *harināma-saṅkīrtana*. This *harināma* is the treasure, the *prema-dhana,* of Goloka Vṛndāvana, the ecstatic sporting *dhāma* of the most attractive supreme personalities Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Śyāma, Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma, and Their beloved associates.

*jaya śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya prabhu-nityānanda*  
*śrī-advaita gadādhara śrīvāsādi-gaura-bhakta-vṛnda*

*hare kṛṣṇa hare kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa hare hare*  
*hare rāma hare rāma rāma rāma hare hare*

The youth in Dallas enthusiastically and regularly participate in a wide variety of devotional services, including preaching, preparing and making *bhoga* offerings, serving *prasādam,* reading and distributing your books, worshiping the Deities, teaching the young, organizing festivals (including taking complete charge of your Vyāsa-pūjā festival), visiting other *yātrās* and inviting friends and families from them, conducting lively *nāma-haṭṭa* programs, organizing picnics for devotees, chanting and dancing ecstatically, regularly visiting with the youth in Houston for programs there and hosting them during the programs here, and leading and assisting in so many ways to create, maintain, and promote so many other aspects of your divine mission.

Indeed, the future of the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement is so much brighter with the very attractive, wholehearted, and joyful participation of the youth. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for the very divine, lovingly caring and affectionate mercy that you daily shower upon our youth and the rest of us. Thank you *ananta-koṭi* times for eternally endearing us to you and your followers, and the Deities and Their associates.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are more dear to us than our own lives. Indeed, you are our worshipable deity and our very life and soul. Please bless us that we may always please you and never displease you.

108 times Śrī Śrīmad Śrīla A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda-*kī jaya*!

All glories, all glories to Śrī Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga!!

Your servants’ humble servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kālachandjī Dhāma, Dallas, Texas, USA.  
(written by Nityānanda Dāsa)

## Darwin Australia

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your most precious lotus feet.

Where would we be if you had never come to the Western world and given us the most wonderful teachings of our Lord Kṛṣṇa? We would have surely been lost and lived a most inauspicious life of unhappiness, not knowing who we really are.

But you saved us all, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and for this we are eternally indebted.

We hope we never forget for a moment what you have done for us. With all our energy we will help keep your ISKCON society happy and prosperous by distributing your books so other lost souls can take up this wonderful way of life and become free from the cycle of birth and death.

We want to take care of the devotees nicely throughout their Kṛṣṇa conscious life, through thick and thin, so they will remain strong and healthy in their pursuit of pure devotional service.

Please let us always remain serious and fixed eternally as your servants.  
Your eternal servants at ISKCON Darwin, Australia.

## Denpasar

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please allow us to offer our most humble and obeisances unto Your Divine Grace’s holy lotus feet. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

It feels not so long ago, though it was actually twenty-eight years ago, that some of us happened to contact Your Divine Grace’s great mission here in Indonesia. It was 1980 that we young boys and girls first found Your Divine Grace’s books in the shops. After reading them with the greatest joy, enthusiasm, and eagerness, we always tried to find more of your books in our language.

Later we met with some difficulties here in our efforts to practice Kṛṣṇa consciousness—difficulties you had predicted during your one visit to Indonesia in 1973. But we had full faith in Your Divine Grace’s words, either delivered through your books or through your representatives, who began coming regularly to our country. Instead of stagnating and worrying about our problems, especially the ban on our activities by the Indonesian government, we learned from them and became more mature.

And by Your Divine Grace’s day-to-day watching over us and guiding us from your place in Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s *nitya-līlā* in Kṛṣṇa-loka, that staunch faith in you has produced ripened fruits, especially since 1997. Now the time is coming when there will be great opportunities for us, and also challenges to be more serious and sincere in spreading your mission. These opportunities and challenges are due to the local reformation era, which has seen a relaxation of restrictions on Kṛṣṇa consciousness here.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we understand that without the mercy of Your Divine Grace and your representatives, we are nothing. So please kindly always stay with us here in Bali, which, though part of a Muslim country, is full of elements of Vedic culture.

Now we are hopeful of real success soon. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we believe you would be pleased to see how a new generation of devotees, young spiritual warriors, are carrying on your great mission. Surrendered to your instructions, we are very eager to spread *harināma-saṅkīrtana* to every town and village here in Bali, and hopefully to all of Indonesia.

The devotees in Denpasar, Bali, Indonesia.

## Detroit

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

By the desire of Lord Kṛṣṇa, one hundred twelve years ago, on this auspicious day, Your Divine Grace appeared in order to spread the mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu all around the world and deliver fallen souls like us. Only by your mercy do we have any hope of being saved from clutches of Māyā.

The revealed scriptures declare that the spiritual master should be worshiped like the Supreme Personality of Godhead, and this injunction is obeyed by pure devotees of the Lord. The spiritual master is the most confidential servant of the Lord. Thus let us offer our respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of our spiritual master. \[*Śrī Gurv-aṣṭakam*, Verse 7\]

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, our knowledge is very poor and our senses are very imperfect. As a result, it is not possible for us to have even the slightest knowledge of the absolute region, the spiritual abode, without surrendering ourselves at your lotus feet. Being so merciful, you have established your International Society for Krishna Consciousness throughout the world so that every soul can receive an opportunity to associate with a pure devotee.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is only due to your causeless mercy that we are now blessed with a vibrant team of young devotees who are constantly working wholeheartedly and tirelessly to please you.

First, we would like to report to you that we now have a team of devotees who go regularly to distribute books at airports, festivals, and events. Our book distribution program is growing every day under the leadership of Śrīmān Bhārata Prabhu.

We have also now established a regular *harināma* schedule to spread the holy name in Michigan.

The Ratha-yātrā committee, under the leadership of Śrīmān Veṇu Gopāla Prabhu and Śrīmān Śrīnivāsa Prabhu, is enthusiastically planning to celebrate the biggest Ratha-yātrā this city has ever seen.

Our Bhakti Vṛkṣa program, under the leadership of Śrīmān Gokulacandra Prabhu, is growing and recruiting young, enthusiastic, sincere souls to taste the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness given by you. Recently they invited His Holiness Indradyumna Swami and arranged a very successful program, in which many guests were touched by the love and compassion of your very dear disciple. How can we thank you enough for giving us this wonderful movement—your books, your sincere disciples, the most beautiful Deities Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kuñjavihārī, festivals, and the desire to serve you.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are all struggling in the ocean of material existence. Please hold our hands tightly so that we do not drown. Only by your unmatched compassion and love for all of us will we be able to continue the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Therefore let us with all earnestness pray at your lotus feet so that we may become able to hear your message patiently, follow your instructions with conviction, and bow down at your lotus feet. Please release us from our present causeless unwillingness to serve Lord Kṛṣṇa and His devotees.

We seek the forgiveness and blessings of all your followers.

Your humble and most fallen servants at Devasadan Mandir, Detroit, Michigan, USA.  
(written by Jagad Guru Dāsa)

## Durban

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace! All glories to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rādhānātha, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and Śrī Girirāja, your Deities in Durban, South Africa.

In many ways, your movement is expanding nicely here. What is encouraging is that for many persons this movement is no longer some organization to visit, or to assist now and again as part of their religious or social duty. Rather, for many it has become their way of life—their eternal connection with Kṛṣṇa, by your divine grace.

We are frequently reminded that your devotional influence has been invested in this movement. Just by engaging in the activities of this movement with faith and sincerity, one can go back home, back to Godhead.

So many types of persons join this movement. Sometimes we calculate, debate even, whether this one will stay, whether that one will be trouble, etc. *Tīvreṇa bhakti-yogena*—the devotional force encapsulated in this movement can move anyone to pure devotional service. All that is necessary is faith, loyalty, and a determination to chant without offenses. (So we are sometimes surprised at who stays and who goes.)

Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu asked, “Of all kinds of distress, what is the most painful?”

Śrī Rāmānanda Raya replied, “Apart from separation from the devotee of Kṛṣṇa, I know of no unbearable unhappiness.” \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 8.248\]

We pray never to be separated from your association—this transcendental movement. It would be the greatest suffering condition: to have had a glimpse of the spiritual world and then to have let it go. May we always be bathed in the most auspicious activity:

Then Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu asked, “Out of all auspicious and beneficial activities, which is best for the living entity?”

Rāmānanda Raya replied, “The only auspicious activity is association with the devotees of Kṛṣṇa.” \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 8.251\]

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are your transcendental movement. May we eternally realize this fact by serving ISKCON without offenses.

Hare Kṛṣṇa. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Your servants at ISKCON Durban, KwaZulu-Natal, South Africa.

## East Hartford

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**  
**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

“The Supreme Personality of Godhead is always anxious to elevate the living entities, who are His parts and parcels, and for their special benefit the Lord travels all over the world in the form of self-realized persons like you.”

In this verse from the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (4.22.16), Pṛthu Mahārāja welcomes and glorifies the four Kumāras. This verse also describes you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Out of your extreme and pure love for Kṛṣṇa, you are always in direct contact with Him, working only for His pleasure. Because the Lord is anxious to deliver the fallen souls from illusion, he has sent you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, His dear confidant.

The poor living entities of Kali-yuga are not even aware of their suffering. But knowing that this suffering is due to a want of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, you have written your Bhaktivedanta purports to capture their hearts and attract them back to Kṛṣṇa.

Having forgotten the Lord of the heart, conditioned souls are in a most unnatural and unhealthy condition. But you, like an expert physician, have skillfully created ISKCON centers all over the world to remedy the situation. They are like hospitals ready to admit anyone wanting relief from the pangs of material life. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for accepting us as your patients. Even though we may be in critical condition—blinded by the jaundice of *avidyā*, exhausted by the fever of material existence, stricken by the heart disease of *ahaṅkāra*, and overwhelmed by the cancer of illusion—please do not give up on us. By your mercy only we have hope that if we somehow or other take the medicine of the chanting of the holy names of the Lord that you have so kindly administered—Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare—someday we will be well again. Under your expert guidance we can be reinstated in our healthy and natural position as eternal loving servants of the all-attractive divine couple, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda.

*yathāgadaṁ vīryatamam upayuktaṁ yadṛcchayā*  
*ajānato ’py ātma-guṇaṁ kuryān mantro ’py udāhṛtaḥ*

**If a person unaware of the effective potency of a certain medicine takes that medicine or is forced to take it, it will act even without his knowledge because its potency does not depend on the patient’s understanding. Similarly, even though one does not know the value of chanting the holy name of the Lord, if one chants knowingly or unknowingly, the chanting will be very effective.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **6.2.19)**

*enechi auṣadhi māyā nāśibāro lāgi’*  
*hari-nāma mahā-mantra lao tumi māgi’*

**\[Lord Caitanya said:\] “O conditioned souls who are foolishly sleeping on the lap of the witch Māyā, I have brought an excellent medicine to cure the disease of your illusion. This medicine is called** ***hari-nāma*****. It is My holy name, and by taking this medicine you will get all perfection in life. Therefore, I sincerely request you to please take this medicine that I have personally brought for you.” (*****Aruṇodaya-kīrtana*** **II, 4)**

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for bringing Lord Caitanya’s miraculous medicine to all the diseased people of the world and for establishing ISKCON temples to administer that medicine. How can we ever repay you?

Falling at your divine lotus feet,  
Your servants in East Hartford, Connecticut, USA.  
(written by Jīvanauṣadhi Devī Dāsī)

## Eger

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our most respectful obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to you on this most blessed day of your Vyāsa-pūjā!

You have innumerable transcendental qualities, and it would be most appropriate to glorify all of them on this most auspicious occasion. But let us take only one of these wonderful qualities today: your fearlessness. Thinking about how you preached alone in India in the early days, how you came to the West, how you then preached all over the world and made your ISKCON into a worldwide organization with thousands of devotees and supporters, one can only exclaim, “How brave you were! How fearless!”

In the early 1990s, when your ISKCON started to grow tremendously in Hungary, many young people joined the temple in our small town, Eger. These young people had mostly been living on the streets—not because they did not have a place at their parents’ home, but because they did not want to do anything the society wanted them to do. They didn’t want to become “one of them”; they were looking for something else. They tried to find it in drugs and alcohol, and they couldn’t care less what anyone said about them. They were fearless.

And then they met you. A Hare Kṛṣṇa Festival came to Eger, and we, for the first time, heard about you. We heard that at the age of seventy you had boarded a ship to go to America alone, with forty rupies in your pocket. “How fearless!” we thought. You arrived in America alone, with the message of your spiritual master. You did not care what people would say of your Vaiṣṇava attire; you were not going to change it for their sake. And from beginning to end you preached the same message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, not changing a word. You were not afraid of anything: you were fearless.

You fearlessly preached that we are not this body. Who had ever heard that before in the Western world? You fearlessly preached that we are spirit souls, that we belong to Kṛṣṇa, that we are His servants. And you were fearless when you said we could all be reinstated in our loving relationship with Kṛṣṇa. This science had been known to everyone in India for thousands of years. But who was brave enough to cross the ocean and give that knowledge to the fallen Westerners? It was you alone, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

You became our hero. And you are still our hero, Śrīla Prabhupāda! You had absolute, unflinching faith in Kṛṣṇa and the words of your spiritual master, and that made you fearless.

Being your grandchildren, we should have the same fearlessness. It is you who are behind our every activity in preaching or distributing your books. It is you whose instructions we are following. It is you whose desires we are trying to fulfill when we preach about Kṛṣṇa consciousness to others. “What is the difficulty?” you asked. Yes, what difficulties can be there when we are strictly following our fearless *ācārya,* our dear spiritual grandfather, our hero?

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg you to give us a particle of that fearlessness, which is one of your most wonderful transcendental qualities. Please make us as brave as you were by giving us your constant association through your books and teachings. We still do not want to become “one of them.” We want to follow you, the bravest spiritual saint of all times, who saved us from the slaughterhouse of materialism.

Your grateful servants in ISKCON Eger, Hungary.

## Gainesville

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful *daṇḍavat-praṇāmas* at your lotus feet.

Your *mūrti* here in Gainesville, Florida—a place you described when you visited in 1971 as “a remote corner of the world, far removed from the birthplace of Lord Caitanya”is one of the most beautiful *mūrtis* anywhere. For many years it has graced the modest Gainesville temple room.

During these years, however, very few new devotees have joined the Gainesville *yātrā*—a troubling trend in America, and especially for a college preaching center such as ours. Few devotees have joined despite the fact that we have been consistently distributing *prasādam* and chanting on campus at the same place you spoke in 1971, just as you instructed us to do.

Last year the temple leaders decided to try some new approaches to attracting devotees. As one devotee pointed out, to do the same thing and expect a different result is a symptom of madness. Bold steps were in order.

Among other things, we proposed to move your *mūrti* from the temple room into a separate room, in which we would establish a Bhaktivedanta Library. This would free temple room space for guests and create a private space for worshiping you, an affair too intimate, we felt, for our young guests.

Some veteran devotees objected that moving your large *mūrti* from the temple room was inauspicious and that you would be displeased. But our GBC approved the plan. So we purchased bookshelves and extra books and moved you in on your Vyāsa-pūjā day. To our surprise, the local newspaper published a picture story about the new library—a good omen.

We held daily *guru-pūjā* in the new library. You seemed pleased. Soon, the library doubled as a meeting and counseling room. Through your *mūrti*, it seemed, you were now more personally involved with temple affairs. And the spot on the temple room floor where your *vyāsāsana* used to sit began filling up with new guests.

Then, just like that, you began sending new devotees—first new *bhaktas* and *bhaktins,* and then experienced devotees to help train them. Today, less than a year later, there are ten new *bhaktas* and *bhaktins* in Gainesville. Spiritually, they’re as wobbly as newborn colts, but they’re all chanting enthusiastically and advancing under the care of senior devotees.

Now we have a new problem: *guru-pūjā* in the library is too crowded. But each day, as the *bhaktas* and *bhaktins* awkwardly step forward to offer you flowers and obeisances, you look even more radiant than before.

The other day a shy new *bhaktin* lead the *guru-pūjā kīrtana*. You appeared to beam through your *mūrti*. Later this quiet young lady said that after leading, she too felt happy all day.

These new devotees are like your representatives—they enliven and challenge me every day. They make me marvel at the extraordinarily patient and brilliant way you dealt with your *bhaktas* and *bhaktins*. They show in a fresh new way how you indeed built a house in which the whole world can live, and how your message is more viable and urgent than ever.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your kindness and courage and grace. May your fame be spread all over the three worlds.

Your loving servants at ISKCON Gainesville, Florida, USA.  
(written by Kalakaṇṭha Dāsa)

## Ghana

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of SarasvatīGosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the true well-wishing friend of all living beings in this world, for you kindly came down to this horrible world to give us information on the transcendental world of Kṛṣṇa, our eternal home.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the mercy incarnation of the Kṛṣṇa, for you kindly made Kṛṣṇa’s mercy available in this world by spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the world for the benefit all living beings.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the most magnanimous devotee of the Lord, for you sacrificed everything in your life for the benefit of others.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the most dynamic preacher of this era, because at a very advanced age you circulated the globe fourteen times just to give Kṛṣṇa consciousness to conditioned souls of this world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are compassion personified, because out of your deep compassion for suffering humanity you established the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement to afford everyone the opportunity to escape the pangs of material miseries.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the most learned *ācārya* of this era, for in the mood of the Six Gosvāmīs you churned the ocean of Vedic wisdom and turned out so many books for the benefit of humanity.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the most exalted Vaiṣṇava of this era, because you did what no one else has ever done: you changed the world in such a short time by your pure and fearless preaching.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are tolerance personified, for you lovingly tolerated so much inconvenience in order to establish and nourish your ISKCON movement to maturity.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are a superman, for you created such a wonderful movement, with so many wonderful devotees, who are wonderfully spreading Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission all over the world in very wonderful and inconceivable ways.

On this most auspicious day of your divine appearance, we pray that you kindly empower us here in Ghana to be better servants of your devotees and carriers of your sublime message in its purest form.

The devotees in Ghana.  
(written by Śrīvāsa Dāsa)

## Gītā-nāgarī

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

In 1976, you first came to the Gītā-nāgarī farm and expressed your desires for developing this farm. “Gītā-nāgarī” had been a dream of yours for a *very* long time, and you were very happy with what you saw here. By Kṛṣṇa’s arrangement and your mercy, all of us here at Gītā-nagarī are being allowed to help fulfill that dream. Thank you for allowing us to serve you in this very special project. Your visit here many years ago has left a lasting impression that is felt by all who come here.

No doubt living here and serving you is no ordinary thing. We just pray that you will always inspire us to keep this dream alive and develop it to your complete satisfaction.

The dust of your lotus feet forever graces this land. Please allow us to always remain your most humble servants.

The residents of Gītā-nāgarī Dhāma, Pennsylvania, USA.  
(written by Locana Padma Devī Dāsī)

## Guyana

Our dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Kindly accept our prostrated obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace and the *paramparā*! All glories to Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and His associates! All glories to our most beautiful Deities, Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulachandra and Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Caitanyachandra.

On this most auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we wish to express our eternal gratitude to you for bringing us the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and for saving us from the degradation of material existence. You indeed give us hope to succeed in fulfilling life’s real goal, i.e., to develop our understanding of and love for the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Without the mercy and blessings of the spiritual master and the Vaiṣṇavas, we cannot advance and get the kind reciprocation of the Supreme Lord.

Every year we proclaim our faith in you and our dedication to you, declaring our intention to follow the many things we need to do to ensure the tangible progress of our Society. But we are beginning to see how far we are from accomplishing this with any great degree of confidence. Moreover, we are experiencing how fallen and conditioned we are and how difficult it is to get our lives together and work with one another harmoniously.

Our most compassionate Śrīla Prabhupāda, we want to use whatever abilities we possess in your service. We also wish to make a sincere prayer that you kindly bestow upon us your causeless mercy and compassion for our individual and collective spiritual perfection. Please instill in us the proper intelligence to guide us on the true path of Kṛṣṇa consciousness—the way you intended it to be. As we endeavor on this path, we can expect to feel confident in receiving your inspiration. This we will consider our most cherished accomplishment.

Dear master and savior, apart from recognizing our insignificant position and many shortcomings, we also recognize the great need for making a deliberate effort to acknowledge what a great personality you are, how dear you are to the Lord, and how much our lives depend on your kind reciprocation.

Begging continuously for your blessings, and praying that however insignificant we are we can be of some meaningful service to ISKCON, we remain

Your servants in Guyana.  
(written by Bhūtādi Prabhu)

## Hare Krishna Dhāma

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

It is said that on the higher planets the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* contains more cantos than we mere humans are familiar with. The demigods must be reading of your wonderful exploits somewhere in the fifteenth or sixteenth canto. For nowhere on this planet or others in this time or any previous time in history has there been a greater preacher. You have done practically single-handedly what others have not even considered to be possible. Other preachers in the Gauḍīya mission thought Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s prediction that Kṛṣṇa’s holy name would be spread to every town and village was some type of figurative statement. But in the short span of twelve years you made it a reality, at an age when most people think only of retirement. Because of you and only you, thousands of people all over the world have become devotees of Kṛṣṇa, dedicating their lives to His mission while following in your footsteps. Now Kṛṣṇa devotees are being made, temples opened, and books distributed in places you did not have the opportunity to visit. Houston was never blessed by Your Divine Grace’s lotus feet, but by your mercy a multimillion-dollar temple complex is currently under construction here.

You told us that ISKCON will go down in history as having saved the entire world. This means *you* will have saved the entire world. In the past and now they build statues and memorials to mundane political leaders, structures that are often later torn down amidst the cheers of a new regime. Perhaps one day they will tear down Mount Rushmore or the statue of Sam Houston to erect a statue of Your Divine Grace.

Even in our short lifetime we have witnessed the effect of your books on society. In the ’60s to be a vegetarian or to believe in reincarnation was considered radical. Now such beliefs and practices are widely accepted. There is even a hit TV show here in America all about a man trying to right his bad karma.

As the golden age within Kali-yuga progresses, people will more and more recognize you greatness. Then people will no longer be distressed by or have to debate key issues such as abortion, evolution, creationism, capital punishment, and the afterlife, because all the answers are in your books. When people read you books they will realize your greatness, and they will make them the law books for all mankind. Please make us your empowered servants to help make this a reality.

Your servants in Hare Krishna Dhama, Houston, Texas, USA.  
(written by Jaya Baladeva Dāsa)

## Honduras

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Extreme mercy and tolerance go together. If someone is very merciful, it is because he is very tolerant, and if someone is very tolerant, it is because he is very merciful. Once a disciple asked you for your mercy, and you replied that you had given him eighty books; wasn’t that mercy? You said that if the devotee would read them and practice what was in them, his life would be perfect.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you had to tolerate so many things while beginning and expanding ISKCON. The *Śrīla Prabhupāda-līlāmṛta* describes how some foolish disciples wanted to diminish your position in the movement to take charge themselves. To find a sane person in Kali-yuga is very difficult. Let’s pray for an ISKCON made up of very merciful and tolerant people. In Kali-yuga there is no humility, compassion, or respect for one another. If the members of ISKCON are able to show to human society that we are humble, compassionate, and tolerant people who are respectful to one and all, the Society will serve as a great example for people in general.

The devotees of ISKCON Honduras.

## Hong Kong

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of SarasvatīGosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet, and please allow us to express our sincere thanks to you. Because of your kindness, we have the opportunity to gain self-realization and practice Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Because of your efforts, we are able to associate with devotees from all over the world through ISKCON. Because of your mercy, we can take shelter at your lotus feet.

The devotees here at ISKCON Hong Kong are overjoyed at having the opportunity to read your books. As you once said, “There’s no difference between my words and myself”; so whenever we read your books, we fell that we are associating with you. O dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, in this way we always endeavor to remind ourselves about our intimate relationship with Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, beloved servant of Lord Kṛṣṇa, we beg for you mercy. Please allow us to serve you, to please you, and to please our spiritual masters and Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi. Only by Their mercy may we somehow manage to render some meager service by doing book distribution and *saṅkīrtana* with other devotees on the streets of Hong Kong.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, O *kalpa-vṛkṣa,* O great servant of the lotus feet of the Lord! We sincerely beg your kind forgiveness for all the offenses we have committed unto the holy name, the devotees, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the holy land, and the scriptures. We beg your mercy so we may awaken from ignorance and be able to remember our role as Lord Kṛṣṇa’s humble servants; in this way we can serve you, the Lord, the spiritual masters, and all devotees.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, O ocean of mercy, lord and master of the fallen souls! Please kindly continue to guide us so we may please you. Please tell us how we can focus our hearts and minds on the lotus feet of Lord Kṛṣṇa. And please bless us so that we may continue your mission of spreading the holy names of Lord Caitanya to the whole world.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda! Hare Kṛṣṇa.

With much love and affection,  
Your humble servants at ISKCON Hong Kong.

## Las Vegas

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances in dust of your lotus feet! All glories to you and your divine mission of spreading Lord Kṛṣṇa’s glories all over the world!

Writing a Vyāsa-pūjā offering for you is always a big inspiration for me to remember you and see how everything in my life depends on your divine mercy.

I remember when, in 1980 as a twenty-year-old young man, I became attracted to your movement by your mercy. At that time, after searching for answers to basic questions concerning life and the Absolute, I concluded that there was no source of knowledge that would give me sufficient explanations. Many people were talking about many different things, but their everyday life contradicted what they were saying. So, I wanted to see if the philosophy you preached worked. After attending three *bhakti-yoga* programs, I joined the temple with the intention of checking so see if the devotees followed their teachings in a real life.

Surprisingly, as soon as the door to your temple opened, I immediately felt a very unique atmosphere that overwhelmed me completely. The mystical spirituality in the temple and the gentleness of the devotees astonished me in a way that had never happened before in my life.

I put my suitcase on the floor and said to the devotee who greeted me, “Ok, please teach me what I should do.” The very first day I started chanting sixteen rounds of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* and became completely ecstatic. After this experiment, I realized not only that devotees follow what they have learned from you and what they preach on the basis of your teachings, but also that I could personally feel what they were talking about! So on the very first day I decided in my heart, This is my life! This is what I want to do with for the rest of my life!

Now I am almost fifty years old, and I pray constantly every day: “Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please let me achieve consciousness purified by the words emanating from your lotus mouth! Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please let me become a useful tool in your hands so that you can manipulate me as you want for the benefit of your preaching mission! Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help me be always totally open for your mercy.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your mercy is my life, your mercy is my shelter and protection. By your divine mercy everything is possible! By your divine mercy it was even possible to establish a temple here in the heart of the capital of Māyā’s kingdom, Las Vegas. By your divine mercy we have opened the first official ISKCON of Las Vegas preaching center, called “Govinda’s Center of Vedic India.” It is located in a very central and beautiful place in Las Vegas, close to the main park, where by your divine mercy we will organize big Hare Kṛṣṇa festivals in the near future.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please continue blessing us with your divine mercy so that we can dynamically and effectively spread the glories of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa all over Las Vegas and in this way satisfy you and Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Your servants in Las Vegas, Nevada, USA.  
(written by Surapāla Dāsa)

## Lome

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my respectful obeisances at your divine lotus feet on this momentous occasion celebrating your appearance on earth.

It is that time of year when we, your grand-disciples, have the opportunity to address Your Divine Grace and report on our preaching efforts and the progress of our Kṛṣṇa conscious activities, which we have been performing according to the instructions we have received from your empowered representatives. Without their dedication and loyalty to you, we would not have the good fortune of being counted among your followers.
